In conformity with the instructions contained in your Circular NO. 38 j, Second Series, I now transmi to the Statistical Department at Shanghai, for publication, the statistice of the trade at Kowloon and the neighbourin stations in Native vessels for the frst quarter of the year i9oo, January-March. b. The Custom House A ) was opened on the 2nd April 1887, and the collection of Provinci ikin and Ching-fei Tax on General Cargo, at the rates fxed by the ProviDneial Tariff received from the Viceroy o the Two Kwang, began on that day.. The collection of Tariff Impo4 Duty and Convention Likin on Opini (1Hk.Ta 30 and Hhh 8o per picul) commenced on the 14th April 1887, and the collecion of Native Duties a General Cargo, at the rates fixed by the Native CustOm House Tariff received from the Hoppo, on the ist July 188 he stations then controlled by the Kowloon Onstoms were known as Capsimoon Changebow ( thow (fh Ifg ), and Kowloon (* I). On the 5th October 1899, as a result of the extension of territor to fornpart of the Colony of Hoigkong, they were removed to Taishan (* f), Lintin (p f), and Shamchu on the north and west; to Samun ( o),on the east; and to Sbhaiichung () on the Mirs Ba littoral. The Custom House is now called M c. The word &quot;Inwards&quot; in these statistics neans to China, and the word &quot; Outwards,&quot; from China. II.
